## Vessel Pollution and Dangerous Chemicals Regulations {#vessel-pollution-and-dangerous-chemicals-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2012-69
:::

[CANADA SHIPPING ACT, 2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15)

Registration 2012-03-30

Vessel Pollution and Dangerous Chemicals Regulations

P.C. 2012-349 2012-03-29
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Whereas the proposed Regulations set out standards that are additional
or complementary to the standards set out in the International
Convention for the Prevention of Pollution from Ships, 1973 and the
Protocols of 1978 and 1997 relating to the Convention, and the Governor
in Council is satisfied that those additional or complementary standards
meet the objectives of the Convention and Protocols;

Therefore, His Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the
recommendation of the Minister of Transport and the Minister of Natural
Resources, pursuant to subsections 7(2), 35(1)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_a){#fn_a-ID0EGACA} and 120(1) and (2),
paragraph 182(a), section 190 and paragraphs 207(2)(a) and 244(a) of the
[Canada Shipping Act, 2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_b){#fn_b-ID0EDACA}, hereby makes the annexed
[Vessel Pollution and Dangerous Chemicals
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2012-69).

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_a-ID0EGACA){#fn_a}S.C.
  2005, c. 29, s. 16(1)
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_b-ID0EDACA){#fn_b}S.C.
  2001, c. 26
  :::
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-789706 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in these Regulations.

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Canada Shipping Act,
      2001](/eng/acts/C-10.15). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[anti-fouling system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[anti-fouling system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a coating,
      paint, surface treatment, surface or device that is used on a
      vessel to control or prevent the attachment of unwanted
      organisms. ([système antisalissure]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Anti-fouling Systems Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Anti-fouling Systems Convention]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      International Convention on the Control of Harmful Anti-Fouling
      Systems on Ships, 2001. ([Convention sur le contrôle des systèmes
      antisalissure]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[arctic waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[arctic waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      section 2 of the [Arctic Waters Pollution Prevention
      Act](/eng/acts/A-12). ([eaux arctiques]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[a similar stage of construction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[a similar stage of construction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      stage at which

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} construction identifiable with a specific
        vessel begins; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} assembly of that vessel reaches the lesser of
        50 tonnes and 1% of the estimated mass of all structural
        material. ([la construction se trouve à un stade
        équivalent]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[BCH Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[BCH Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Code for the
      Construction and Equipment of Ships Carrying Dangerous Chemicals
      in Bulk, published by the IMO. ([Recueil BCH]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Marine Technical Review
      Board established under section 26 of the
      Act. ([Bureau]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Canadian pleasure craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Canadian pleasure craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a pleasure
      craft that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is licensed under Part 10 of the Act; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is principally maintained or operated in
        Canada, is not a Canadian vessel and is not registered or
        licensed under the laws of another state. ([embarcation de
        plaisance canadienne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[cargo residues]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cargo residues]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the remnants of any
      cargo material on board a vessel that cannot be placed in proper
      cargo holds (loading excess and spillage) or that remain in cargo
      holds or elsewhere after unloading procedures are completed
      (unloading residual and spillage) and includes cargo
      sweepings. ([résidus de cargaison]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[cargo vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cargo vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel that is not a
      passenger vessel or a pleasure craft. ([bâtiment de
      charge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[carried in packaged form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[carried in packaged form]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means carried in
      a receptacle that has a net mass not exceeding 400 kg or a
      capacity not exceeding 450 L. ([transporté en
      colis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Certificate of Fitness for an Offshore Support Vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Certificate of Fitness for an Offshore Support
      Vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a Certificate of Fitness
      referred to in Resolution A.673(16). ([certificat d'aptitude pour
      un bâtiment de servitude au large]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[chemical tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[chemical tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel that was
      constructed or adapted for the carriage in bulk of any dangerous
      chemical. ([bâtiment-citerne pour produits
      chimiques]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[combination carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[combination carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel
      designed to carry oil or solid cargoes in bulk. ([transporteur
      mixte]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[crude oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[crude oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any liquid hydrocarbon
      mixture occurring naturally in the earth, whether or not treated
      to render it suitable for transportation, and includes

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} crude oil from which distillate fractions have
        been removed; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} crude oil to which distillate fractions have
        been added. ([pétrole brut]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[crude oil tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[crude oil tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an oil tanker
      engaged in the trade of carrying crude oil. ([transporteur de
      pétrole brut]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[dangerous chemical]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[dangerous chemical]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any liquid
      substance listed in chapter 17 of the IBC Code. ([produit chimique
      dangereux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[deadweight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[deadweight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the difference in tonnes
      between the displacement of a vessel in water of a specific
      gravity of 1.025 at the load waterline corresponding to the
      assigned summer freeboard and the lightweight of the
      vessel. ([port en lourd]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[emission control area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[emission control area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 110.3,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the North American Emission Control Area, and

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the United States Caribbean Sea area
          described in Appendix VII to Annex VI to MARPOL; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 111,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the Baltic Sea area, as defined in regulation
          1.11.2 of Annex I to MARPOL,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the North Sea area, as defined in regulation
          5(1)(f) of Annex V to MARPOL,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the North American Emission Control Area,
          and

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the United States Caribbean Sea area
          described in Appendix VII to Annex VI to MARPOL. ([zone de
          contrôle des émissions]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[en route]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[en route]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a vessel, means
      being underway on a course that, so far as feasible for
      navigational purposes, will cause any discharge to be spread over
      as great an area as is reasonably feasible. ([fait
      route]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fishing zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fishing zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as a
      fishing zone of Canada described in the Fishing Zones of Canada
      (Zones 1, 2 and 3) Order, the Fishing Zones of Canada (Zones 4
      and 5) Order or the Fishing Zones of Canada (Zone 6) Order. ([zone
      de pêche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[foreign pleasure craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign pleasure craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a pleasure
      craft that is not a Canadian vessel or a Canadian pleasure
      craft. ([embarcation de plaisance étrangère]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[from the nearest land]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[from the nearest land]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means seaward from
      the baseline from which the territorial sea of the territory in
      question is established in accordance with international law,
      except that off the northeastern coast of Australia it has the
      same meaning as in Annexes I, II, IV and V to MARPOL. ([à partir
      de la terre la plus proche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[garbage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[garbage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means all kinds of victual,
      domestic and operational waste that is generated during the normal
      operation of a vessel and that is likely to be disposed of
      continuously or periodically, and includes plastics, dunnage,
      lining and packing materials, galley wastes and refuse such as
      paper products, rags, glass, metal, bottles, crockery, incinerator
      ash and cargo residues. However, it does not include fresh fish,
      fresh fish parts, oil, oily mixtures, noxious liquid substances,
      liquid substances that are listed in chapter 18 of the IBC Code
      and categorized as OS in the Pollution Category column of that
      chapter, liquid substances that are provisionally assessed under
      regulation 6.3 of Annex II to MARPOL as falling outside
      category X, Y or Z, substances listed in Schedule 1, marine
      pollutants, sewage or sewage sludge. ([ordures]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[gas carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gas carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a cargo vessel that was
      constructed or adapted for the carriage in bulk of any liquefied
      gas or other products listed in chapter 19 of the International
      Code for the Construction and Equipment of Ships Carrying
      Liquefied Gases in Bulk, published by the IMO. ([transporteur de
      gaz]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Great Lakes and St. Lawrence waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Great Lakes and St. Lawrence waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      the Great Lakes and the St. Lawrence River and their connecting
      waters, and the Gulf of St. Lawrence to the baseline of the
      territorial sea. ([eaux des Grand Lacs et du fleuve
      Saint-Laurent]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[handling facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[handling facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any shore or sea
      installation that is used for the loading or unloading of oil,
      oily mixtures, noxious liquid substances or dangerous chemicals to
      or from vessels. ([installation de manutention]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[IBC Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[IBC Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the International Code for
      the Construction and Equipment of Ships Carrying Dangerous
      Chemicals in Bulk, published by the IMO. ([Recueil
      IBC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[IMO]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[IMO]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the International Maritime
      Organization. ([OMI]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[inland waters of Canada]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[inland waters of Canada]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, except in
      Division 4 of Part 2, has the same meaning as in section 1 of the
      [Vessel Safety Certificates
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2021-135). ([eaux internes du
      Canada]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[installed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[installed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a marine diesel
      engine, means that the engine is fitted on a vessel, and that the
      engine's cooling or exhaust system is an integral part of the
      vessel or that the engine's fuelling system is permanently affixed
      to the vessel. ([installé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[lightweight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[lightweight]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the displacement of a
      vessel in tonnes without cargo, fuel, lubricating oil, ballast
      water, fresh water and feed water in tanks, consumable stores, or
      passengers and crew and their effects. ([poids
      lège]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[liquid substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[liquid substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substance that is
      in liquid form and has a vapour pressure not exceeding 0.28 MPa
      absolute at a temperature of 37.8°C. ([substance
      liquide]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[machinery spaces]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[machinery spaces]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in section 2 of the [Marine Machinery
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-90-264). ([tranche des
      machines]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[major conversion]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[major conversion]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in the case of a
      vessel referred to in Division 1, 2, 4 or 6 of Part 2, a
      conversion of a vessel that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} substantially alters the dimensions or carrying
        capacity of the vessel;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} changes the type of the vessel;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is intended to substantially prolong the life
        of the vessel; or

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} alters the vessel such that it becomes subject
        to any provision of that Division that would not be applicable
        to it otherwise. ([transformation importante]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[marine diesel engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[marine diesel engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any
      reciprocating internal combustion engine operating on liquid or
      dual fuel, including any booster system or compound
      system. ([moteur diesel marin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[marine pollutants]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[marine pollutants]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      [[harmful substances]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in regulation 1 of
      Annex III to MARPOL, and includes packaging that has been used for
      the carriage of a harmful substance unless adequate precautions
      have been taken to ensure that the packaging contains no residue
      that is harmful to the marine environment. ([polluants
      marins]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[marine sanitation device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[marine sanitation device]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any
      equipment that is installed on a vessel and is designed to receive
      and treat sewage. ([appareil d'épuration marine]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[MARPOL]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[MARPOL]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the International Convention
      for the Prevention of Pollution from Ships, 1973 and the Protocols
      of 1978 and 1997 relating to the
      Convention. ([MARPOL]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of
      Transport. ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[NLS tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[NLS tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel constructed or
      adapted to carry a cargo of noxious liquid substances in bulk and
      includes an oil tanker that is certified to carry a cargo or part
      cargo of noxious liquid substances in bulk. ([bâtiment-citerne
      SLN]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[North American Emission Control Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[North American Emission Control Area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      the North American area described in Appendix VII to Annex VI to
      MARPOL. ([zone de contrôle des émissions de l'Amérique du
      Nord]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[NO~x~ Technical Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[NO~x~ Technical Code]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Technical
      Code on Control of Emission of Nitrogen Oxides from Marine Diesel
      Engines, published by the IMO. ([Code technique sur les
      NO~x~]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[noxious liquid substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[noxious liquid substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a liquid
      substance, alone or in a mixture with other substances, that is
      listed in chapter 17 or 18 of the IBC Code and categorized as
      Category X, Y or Z in the Pollution Category column of the chapter
      in which it is listed, or is provisionally assessed under
      regulation 6.3 of Annex II to MARPOL as falling into Category X, Y
      or Z. ([substance liquide nocive]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[offshore support vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[offshore support vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel to
      which Resolution A.673(16) applies. ([bâtiment de servitude au
      large]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[oil tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[oil tanker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel constructed or
      adapted primarily to carry oil in bulk in its cargo spaces and
      includes a combination carrier, an NLS tanker and a gas carrier
      that is carrying a cargo or part cargo of oil in
      bulk. ([pétrolier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[oily mixture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[oily mixture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mixture with any oil
      content. ([mélange d'hydrocarbures]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ozone-depleting substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ozone-depleting substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      controlled substance as defined in paragraph 4 of article 1 of the
      Montreal Protocol on Substances that Deplete the Ozone
      Layer. ([substance qui appauvrit la couche
      d'ozone]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[passenger vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[passenger vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vessel that
      carries more than 12 passengers. ([bâtiment à
      passagers]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[plastics]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[plastics]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} synthetic ropes, synthetic fishing nets and
        plastic garbage bags; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} incinerator ash that is from plastics and may
        contain toxic or heavy metal residues. ([matières
        plastiques]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ppm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ppm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means parts per million, by
      volume. ([ppm]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[residual chlorine content]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[residual chlorine content]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      quantity of free available chlorine determined to be in effluent
      when it is tested in accordance with the amperometric titration
      method described in section 4500-Cl D of the Standard
      Methods. ([chlore résiduel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Resolution A.673(16)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Resolution A.673(16)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[[Resolution
      A.673(16)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Guidelines for the
      Transport and Handling of Limited Amounts of Hazardous and Noxious
      Liquid Substances in Bulk on Offshore Support Vessels, IMO
      Resolution A.673(16). ([résolution A.673(16)]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Resolution MEPC.107(49)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Resolution MEPC.107(49)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[[Resolution
      MEPC.107(49)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Revised Guidelines
      and Specifications for Pollution Prevention Equipment for
      Machinery Space Bilges of Ships, the Annex to IMO Resolution
      MEPC.107(49). ([résolution MEPC.107(49)]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Resolution MEPC.184(59)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Resolution MEPC.184(59)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[[Resolution
      MEPC.184(59)]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Annex to the 2009
      Guidelines for Exhaust Gas Cleaning Systems, IMO Resolution
      MEPC.184(59). ([résolution MEPC.184(59)]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Section I waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Section I waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing zone 1,
      fishing zone 2, fishing zone 3 and

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of Division 1 of Part 2, any
        other portion of the internal waters of Canada that is not in
        arctic waters; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of Divisions 4, 5 and 7 of
        Part 2, any other portion of the internal waters of Canada that
        is not in a shipping safety control zone. ([eaux de la
        section I]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Section II waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Section II waters]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means waters under
      Canadian jurisdiction that are not in

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fishing zone 1, fishing zone 2, fishing zone 3
        or any other portion of the internal waters of Canada; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} arctic waters. ([eaux de la
        section II]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[segregated ballast]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[segregated ballast]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means ballast water
      that is introduced into a tank that is completely separated from
      the cargo-oil system and oil-fuel system and permanently allocated
      to the carriage of ballast or to the carriage of ballast and
      cargoes other than oil, noxious liquid substances or substances
      listed in Schedule 1. ([ballast séparé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[sewage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sewage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} human body wastes and wastes from other living
        animals;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} drainage and other wastes from toilets and
        other receptacles intended to receive or retain human body
        wastes;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} drainage from medical premises such as a
        dispensary or a sick bay via wash basins, wash tubs and scuppers
        located in such premises;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} drainage from spaces containing living animals;
        and

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} other drainage or wastes when mixed with the
        drainage or other wastes referred to in paragraph (a), (b), (c)
        or (d). ([eaux usées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[shipboard incinerator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[shipboard incinerator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a shipboard
      facility designed for the primary purpose of incineration on board
      a vessel of wastes or other matter generated during the normal
      operation of the vessel. ([incinérateur de bord]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[shipping safety control zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[shipping safety control zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in section 2 of the [Arctic Waters Pollution Prevention
      Act](/eng/acts/A-12). ([zone de contrôle de la sécurité de la
      navigation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sludge oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sludge oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means sludge from the fuel oil
      or lubricating oil separators, waste lubricating oil from main or
      auxiliary machinery, and waste oil from bilge water separators,
      oil filtering equipment or drip trays. ([boues
      d'hydrocarbures]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[smoke]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[smoke]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any solid, liquid, gas or
      combination of them produced by the combustion of fuel, and
      includes particulate matter. ([fumée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Standard Methods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Standard Methods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Standard
      Methods for the Examination of Water and Wastewater, jointly
      published by the American Public Health Association, the American
      Water Works Association and the Water Environment
      Federation. ([Standard Methods]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[transfer operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[transfer operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of Division 1 of Part 2,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the loading of oil or an oily mixture in bulk
          to a vessel from a handling facility or another vessel, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the unloading of oil or an oily mixture in
          bulk from a vessel to a handling facility or another vessel;
          and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of Division 2 of Part 2,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the loading of a noxious liquid substance or
          dangerous chemical in bulk to a vessel from a handling
          facility or another vessel, or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the unloading of a noxious liquid substance
          or dangerous chemical in bulk from a vessel to a handling
          facility or another vessel. ([opération de
          transbordement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[waters under Canadian jurisdiction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[waters under Canadian jurisdiction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Canadian waters; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} waters in the exclusive economic zone of
        Canada. ([eaux de compétence canadienne]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date vessel is constructed

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, a vessel is
  constructed on the earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which its keel is laid, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which a similar stage of construction is
    reached.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorized representative

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, a reference to
  the authorized representative of a pleasure craft that is not a
  Canadian vessel is to be read as a reference to the operator of the
  pleasure craft.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificates

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, a reference to
  a certificate that a vessel is required to hold and keep on board is
  to be read as a reference to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the vessel is a Canadian vessel or a Canadian
    pleasure craft, a certificate issued under these Regulations; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the vessel is a foreign vessel or a foreign
    pleasure craft, a certificate issued in English, French or Spanish
    by or on behalf of the government of the state whose flag the vessel
    is entitled to fly.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Referenced documents --- as amended from
time to time

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  as otherwise indicated in these Regulations, any reference in these
  Regulations to a document is a reference to the document as amended
  from time to time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Administration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, wherever the
  term "Administration" appears in a document incorporated by reference
  into these Regulations, it means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a Canadian vessel or a Canadian
    pleasure craft, the Minister; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a foreign vessel or a foreign
    pleasure craft, the government of the state whose flag the vessel is
    entitled to fly.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inconsistencies

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the event of an inconsistency between a definition
  in a document incorporated by reference into these Regulations and any
  other definition in these Regulations, that other definition prevails
  to the extent of the inconsistency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Should

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of interpreting a document
  incorporated by reference into these Regulations, "should" is to be
  read as "must".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Footnotes

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, guidelines,
  recommendations, requirements and similar matters set out in a
  document referred to in a footnote to a document incorporated by
  reference into these Regulations are to be considered mandatory.

## [Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-789840 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Except
  as otherwise provided, these Regulations apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} vessels in waters under Canadian jurisdiction; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Canadian vessels everywhere.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vessels engaged in exploration or
  drilling

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations apply in respect of vessels that
  are capable of engaging in the drilling for, or the production,
  conservation or processing of, oil or gas, except when the vessel is
  on location and engaged in the exploration or drilling for, or the
  production, conservation or processing of, oil or gas as defined in
  section 2 of the [Canada Oil and Gas Operations Act](/eng/acts/O-7),
  in an area described in paragraph 3(a) or (b) of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Government vessels

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Sections 187 and 189 of the Act and these Regulations
  do not apply in respect of government vessels.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Vessels owned or operated by a foreign
  state

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations, other than sections 5, 30, 31, 101
  and 102, do not apply in respect of vessels that are owned or operated
  by a foreign state when they are being used only in government
  non-commercial service.

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-789852 .Part}

### [Pollutants]{.HTitleText2} {#h-789853 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed pollutants

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
sections 187 and 189 of the Act, the following substances are prescribed
pollutants:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} oil and any oily mixture;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} garbage; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} organotin compounds that act as biocides.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions to prohibited discharges

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of section
187 of the Act and sections 7, 29, 67, 82, 95, 100 and 126, substances
may be discharged, and for the purposes of subsection 109(1) and section
110.6, substances may be emitted, if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the discharge or emission is necessary for the
  purpose of saving lives, securing the safety of a vessel or preventing
  the immediate loss of a vessel;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the discharge or emission occurs as a result of an
  accident of navigation in which a vessel or its equipment is damaged,
  unless the accident occurs as a result of an action that is outside
  the ordinary practice of seafarers;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the discharge is a minimal and unavoidable leakage of
  oil that occurs as a result of the operation of an underwater
  machinery component;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the discharge is an accidental loss of a synthetic
  fishing net and all reasonable precautions were taken to prevent the
  loss;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the discharge is a discharge of garbage that results
  from damage to a vessel or its equipment, and all reasonable
  precautions were taken

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} before the occurrence of the damage to prevent and
    minimize the discharge, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} after the occurrence of the damage to minimize the
    discharge; or

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the emission involves pollution of the air and
  results from damage to a vessel or its equipment, and all reasonable
  precautions were taken

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} before the occurrence of the damage to prevent and
    minimize the emission, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} after the occurrence of the damage to minimize the
    emission.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-68, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20120330.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Detection of Violations and Enforcement of MARPOL]{.HTitleText2} {#h-789873 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Article 6 of MARPOL

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may board a foreign vessel and take action under Article 6 of
  MARPOL, including inspecting the vessel for the purpose of paragraph 5
  of that Article.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}If MARPOL does not apply

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a foreign vessel to which MARPOL does
  not apply, Article 6 applies as though MARPOL applied to the vessel.

### [Canadian Vessels in Special Areas]{.HTitleText2} {#h-789879 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Oil and oily mixtures

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  Canadian vessel, and a person on a Canadian vessel, must not discharge
  oil or an oily mixture in any of the following areas except in
  accordance with the requirements of regulations 15 and 34 of Annex I
  to MARPOL or in the circumstances set out in section 5 that apply in
  respect of the discharge:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Mediterranean Sea area, as defined in
    regulation 1.11.1 of Annex I to MARPOL;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Baltic Sea area, as defined in
    regulation 1.11.2 of Annex I to MARPOL;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Black Sea area, as defined in regulation 1.11.3
    of Annex I to MARPOL;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the Gulfs area, as defined in regulation 1.11.5 of
    Annex I to MARPOL;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the Antarctic area, as defined in regulation 1.11.7
    of Annex I to MARPOL;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the North West European waters, as defined in
    regulation 1.11.8 of Annex I to MARPOL;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the Southern South African waters, as defined in
    regulation 1.11.10 of Annex I to MARPOL; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} Arctic waters, as defined in regulation 46.2 of
    Annex I to MARPOL.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Noxious liquid substances

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A Canadian vessel, and a person on a Canadian vessel,
  must not discharge a noxious liquid substance in the waters south of
  60°S or in arctic waters as defined in regulation 21.2 of Annex II to
  MARPOL, except in the circumstances set out in section 5 that apply in
  respect of the discharge.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Garbage

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A Canadian vessel, and a person on a Canadian vessel,
  must not discharge garbage in any of the following areas that are not
  under Canadian jurisdiction, except in accordance with the
  requirements of regulation 5(2) of Annex V to MARPOL or in the
  circumstances set out in section 5 that apply in respect of the
  discharge:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} special areas, as defined in regulation 1.14 of
    Annex V to MARPOL; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Arctic waters, as defined in regulation 5.2 of
    Annex V to MARPOL.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-286, s. 31
:::

s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

### [Equipment]{.HTitleText2} {#h-789900 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  authorized representative of a Canadian vessel or a Canadian pleasure
  craft must ensure that any equipment that is referred to in paragraph
  25(2)(a), subsection 93(2), paragraph 111.2(a) or 122(1)(b) or
  subsection 131.1(6) and that is on the vessel

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is of a type approved by the Minister as meeting
    the applicable requirements of these Regulations; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is maintained in good working order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person must not operate equipment referred to in
  subsection (1) that no longer meets the applicable requirements.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-68, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-9-20120330.html "Link to previous version of section 9"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of type approval

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** On application, the
Minister must issue a certificate of type approval for any equipment
referred to in subsection 9(1) if the Minister determines that the
equipment meets the applicable requirements of these Regulations.

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Specific Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-789912 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Oil]{.HTitleText2} {#h-789913 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION 1]{.HLabel3}[Construction and Equipment]{.HTitleText3} {#h-789914 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Plans and specifications

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** On application, the
Minister must approve plans and specifications with respect to a
Canadian vessel or a vessel that is recorded under the Act if the
matters described in the plans and specifications meet the applicable
requirements of this Subdivision and Subdivision 7.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equipment requirements

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  authorized representative of an oil tanker of 150 gross tonnage or
  more, or of any other vessel of 400 gross tonnage or more that carries
  oil as cargo or as fuel, must ensure that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an oil tanker, it

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements of regulations 25.1 to
      25.4 of Annex I to MARPOL respecting the hypothetical outflow of
      oil requirements,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements of regulations 26.2 to
      26.6 of Annex I to MARPOL respecting the size limitation and
      arrangements of cargo tanks,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements of regulations 27, 28.1
      to 28.4 and 28.6 of Annex I to MARPOL respecting subdivision and
      stability,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} has slop tank arrangements that meet the
      requirements of regulation 29 of Annex I to MARPOL,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} is fitted with pumping, piping and discharge
      arrangements that meet the requirements of regulations 30.1 to
      30.4 of Annex I to MARPOL,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} is fitted with an oil discharge monitoring and
      control system that meets the requirements of regulation 31 of
      Annex I to MARPOL, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} is fitted with oil-water interface detectors
      that meet the requirements of regulation 32 of Annex I to MARPOL;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a crude oil tanker of 20 000 tonnes
    deadweight or more but less than 40 000 tonnes deadweight, it meets
    the requirements of paragraph (a) and is fitted with

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} segregated ballast tanks that meet the
      requirements of regulations 18.2 and 18.12 to 18.15 of Annex I to
      MARPOL, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a crude oil washing system and associated
      equipment and arrangements that meet the requirements of
      regulation 33.2 of Annex I to MARPOL;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an oil tanker of 30 000 tonnes
    deadweight or more engaged in carrying oil other than crude oil as
    cargo, it meets the requirements of subparagraph (b)(i) or, if the
    tanker is of 40 000 tonnes deadweight or more, was constructed
    before July 31, 1995 and has not undergone a major conversion since
    that date, it operates with dedicated clean ballast tanks that meet
    the requirements of regulation 18.8 of Annex I to MARPOL;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a crude oil tanker of 40 000 tonnes
    deadweight or more, it is fitted with

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} segregated ballast tanks that meet the
      requirements of regulations 18.2 and 18.12 to 18.15 of Annex I to
      MARPOL, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a crude oil washing system and associated
      equipment and arrangements that meet the requirements of
      regulation 33.2 of Annex I to MARPOL;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vessel of 400 gross tonnage or
    more, unless it engages only on voyages in Section I waters and is
    fitted with a holding tank that has a volume adequate for the
    retention on board of oily bilge water, it is fitted with

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} oil filtering equipment, alarm arrangements and
      automatic stopping arrangements that meet the requirements of
      regulation 14 of Annex I to MARPOL, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if it is of less than 10 000 gross tonnage and
      engages on voyages in Section II waters, oil filtering equipment
      that meets the requirements of regulation 14 of Annex I to MARPOL;
      and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a vessel that engages only on
    voyages in the inland waters of Canada and is not fitted with a
    holding tank that has a volume adequate for the retention on board
    of oily mixtures from the machinery space bilges, it is fitted with
    oil filtering equipment that meets the requirements of regulation 14
    of Annex I to MARPOL, with a 5 ppm bilge alarm that meets the
    requirements of section 13 and with an automatic stopping
    arrangement that is substantially similar to that referred to in
    regulation 14.7 of Annex I to MARPOL.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subparagraph
  (1)(a)(i)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subparagraph (1)(a)(i) does not apply in respect of
  an oil tanker

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for which the building contract is placed after
    December 31, 2006;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is constructed after June 30, 2007, in the
    absence of a building contract;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is delivered after January 9, 2010; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} that undergoes a major conversion

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for which the building contract is placed after
      December 31, 2006,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for which the construction work begins after
      June 30, 2007, in the absence of a building contract, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} that is completed after December 31, 2009.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limited application of subparagraph
  (1)(a)(ii)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subparagraph (1)(a)(ii) applies only in respect of an
  oil tanker referred to in regulation 26.1 of Annex I to MARPOL that is
  not an oil tanker delivered on or after 1 January 2010 as defined in
  regulation 1.28.8 of that Annex.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subparagraphs
  (1)(a)(iv), (vi) and (vii)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subparagraphs (1)(a)(iv), (vi) and (vii) do not apply
  in respect of an oil tanker that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} engages exclusively in carrying cargoes of asphalt
    or similar oils that, through their physical properties, inhibit the
    effective separation of oil and water and effective monitoring of
    the discharge of oil; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} engages only on voyages that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} are in waters under Canadian jurisdiction within
      50 nautical miles from the nearest land, other than in shipping
      safety control zones, and are of 72 hours or less in duration, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} are in shipping safety control zones.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subparagraphs
  (1)(a)(vi) and (vii)

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subparagraphs (1)(a)(vi) and (vii) do not apply in
  respect of an oil tanker that engages only on voyages in waters under
  Canadian jurisdiction within 50 nautical miles from the nearest land
  or in shipping safety control zones.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subparagraphs
  (1)(a)(iv) to (vii)

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subparagraphs (1)(a)(iv) to (vii) do not apply in
  respect of an oil tanker that does not have mechanical means of
  propulsion and cannot wash or ballast its cargo tanks while en route.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limited application of paragraphs
  (1)(b) to (d)

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(b) to (d) apply only in respect of an
  oil tanker delivered after 1 June 1982 as defined in regulation 1.28.4
  of Annex I to MARPOL.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of paragraph (1)(e) in
  shipping safety controls zones

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(e), a vessel of 400 gross
  tonnage or more that engages on voyages in shipping safety control
  zones must be fitted with a holding tank that has a volume adequate
  for the retention on board of oily bilge water. The vessel is required
  to meet the requirements of subparagraph (1)(e)(i) or (ii) only if the
  vessel also engages on voyages in Section II waters.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of paragraphs (1)(e)
  and (f)

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(e) and (f) do not apply in respect of
  a vessel that does not have mechanical means of propulsion or a total
  auxiliary power of 400 kW or more.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Oily-water separating equipment

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(e) and the
  requirements of regulation 14 of Annex I to MARPOL in respect of oil
  filtering equipment, oily-water separating equipment that was
  installed before July 31, 1995 may be used if a process unit that
  meets the requirements of Appendix 1 to the Recommendation Concerning
  the Installation of Oily-Water Separating Equipment Under the
  International Convention for the Prevention of Pollution from Ships,
  1973 as Modified by the Protocol of 1978 Relating Thereto, the Annex
  to IMO Resolution A.444(XI), is attached to the equipment.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}5 ppm bilge alarms

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  5 ppm bilge alarm required under paragraph 12(1)(f) must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} meet the requirements of Part 2 of the Annex to
    Resolution MEPC.107(49);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have an oil content meter that can detect and
    measure 5 ppm or less of oil in a vessel's machinery space bilge
    water overboard discharge;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} have automatic stopping arrangements; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} meet the requirements of section 7 of the Standard
    for 5 ppm Bilge Alarms for Canadian Inland Waters, TP 12301,
    published by Transport Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grandfathering

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(a) and (d) do not apply in respect of
  a 5 ppm bilge alarm installed before May 3, 2007 on a vessel
  constructed before January 1, 2005 if the alarm meets the applicable
  requirements of subparagraph 8(1)(e)(ii) of the [Oil Pollution
  Prevention Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-3) as they read on
  May 2, 2007.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Containers or enclosed deck areas for
bunkering operations

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  authorized representative of a vessel of 100 gross tonnage or more
  must ensure that it is fitted with a container or has an enclosed deck
  area that, under even-keel conditions,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} can retain oil that might leak or spill during
    bunkering operations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has a capacity of not less than 0.08 m^3^ if the
    vessel is of less than 400 gross tonnage or not less than 0.16 m^3^
    if the vessel is of 400 gross tonnage or more; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} does not adversely affect the stability of the
    vessel or the safety of its crew.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a vessel
  that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is fitted with an overflow system that prevents oil
    from overflowing onto the open deck; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} usually fills its bunkers from a truck and is
    equipped with a bunkering hose that has an inside diameter of 51 mm
    or less and employs an automatic shut-off nozzle.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Containers or enclosed deck areas for oil
tankers

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  authorized representative of an oil tanker must ensure that each oil
  cargo manifold and each cargo transfer connection point on the tanker
  is fitted with a container or has an enclosed deck area that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} can retain oil that might leak or spill during
    transfer operations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has a means for the removal of the oil retained in
    it; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} does not adversely affect the stability of the
    tanker or the safety of its crew.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Volume

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the largest conduit serving an oil cargo manifold
  or a cargo transfer connection point on an oil tanker has an inside
  diameter set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection, the
  tanker's authorized representative must ensure that the container or
  enclosed deck area, under even-keel conditions, has the volume set out
  in column 2.

  <figure id="789995">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">

  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top"></th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Inside

  Enclosed Deck Area</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
